EXTRAORDINARY

(Published by Authority)PART I : SECTION (I) — GENERALGovernment Notifications

THE PARLIAMENTARY ELECTIONS ACT, No. 1 OF 1981

Notice Under Section 24(1)

General Elections of Members of the Parliament

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 24(1) of the Parliamentary Elections Act, No. 1 of 1981 that –

(a) the election of Members of the Parliament in each of the Electoral Districts specified in the Schedulehereto, is contested ;

(b) the order in which the name of each recognized Political Party and the distinguishing number of eachIndependent Group and the symbol allotted to each such Party or Group appear in the ballot paper of each such ElectoralDistrict shall be the same order as given in the Schedule hereto ;

(c) the names of candidates (as indicated by the candidates) of each recognized Political Party or IndependentGroup, placed in alphabetical order in accordance with the Sinhala alphabet, nominated for election as Members ofParliament from each such Electoral District and the preferential number assigned to each candidate, are as specified in theSchedule hereto ;

(d) the poll will be taken place between 7.00 a.m. and 4.00 p. m. on 17th August, 2015 in each such electoraldistrict ; and

(e) the situation of the polling station or stations for each of the polling districts in each such Electoral District,and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters, if any, are as specified in the Schedule hereto.

MAHINDA DESHAPRIYA,Elections Secretariat,Commissioner of Elections.Sarana Mawatha,Rajagiriya.13th July, 2015.
